# Common standard checklist

## Kit

| ID | Task | Deliverables | Verification | State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| K240 | The full suite runs only in CI. Cause: the full-run guard allows `make check` when no item is waiting or active, against PRINCIPLES.md, and no tool stops an agent from running a suite. Acceptance: no local command runs the full suite or a suite target, a suite target refuses outside a GitHub Actions run with the command that runs it in CI, and a vendored Claude Code PreToolUse hook refuses a Bash command that runs a suite target or a whole runtime without a selection, naming the command to use instead. | `scripts/kit/full-run.mts`, `scripts/kit/claude-hook.mts`, `.claude/settings.json` (vendored), `kit.mk` | `make kit-test` | [o] |
| K240-1 | The isolation test reads the imports of a module as Node.js reads them (follows K240). Cause: the test found imports with a regular expression over the text, so the option names `'--import'` and `'--require'` in a string of `scripts/kit/test-selection.mts` counted as imports of the specifiers `, ` and failed the rule. Acceptance: the test reads the imports through `parseModule` and `moduleImports` of `scripts/kit/module-imports.mts`, names a computed specifier as one that fails the rule, and passes. | `tests/kit/isolation.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/isolation.test.mts` | [o] |
| K240-2 | The layout allows the folders of the Claude Code settings (follows K240). Cause: K240 added `.claude/` and the source `claude/` at the root, and layout-check refused both in the CI run of 7af0f5c; K240 had not run the owner check of the new paths. Acceptance: the common root folders of `config/layout.kit.yml` hold `.claude`, the place that Claude Code reads (S1), kit's `config/layout.yml` holds `claude` with its reason as it holds `workflows`, and layout-check passes. | `config/layout.kit.yml`, `config/layout.yml` | `make layout-check`, `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/source/layout-real.test.mts` | [o] |
| K248 | Every rule is enforced where it cannot be skipped. Cause: most rules of PRINCIPLES.md and of each AGENTS.md hold only as text, and a check that nobody runs enforces nothing. Acceptance: every rule of PRINCIPLES.md and AGENTS.md ends with `Enforced by:` and a check that a CI workflow, a Git hook or the vendored Claude Code hook runs, `planned <item>` with the open item that builds its check, or `none` with the reason that no tool can enforce it; a kit check (`make rules-check`) follows each named check from the workflows and the hooks through the prerequisites and recipes of make and fails on a rule without the field, a check that no such point runs, a planned item that is missing or done, `none` without a reason, and a Korean twin that names other checks; findings name the file and the line, so a rule needs no ID of its own; the Claude Code hook refuses `--no-verify`, a change of `core.hooksPath` and an edit of a vendored file; kit lists the rules that no tool enforces. | `scripts/kit/check-rules.mts`, `scripts/kit/claude-hook.mts`, `PRINCIPLES.md(.ko)`, `AGENTS.md(.ko)` | `make kit-test` | [o] |
| K248-1 | Every repository runs the rebuild check and the rules check (follows K248). Cause: `make rebuild-check`, the check of S1, was a target of kit's own Makefile only, so no consumer ran it, and the rules check had no target. Acceptance: kit.mk defines `rebuild-check` and `rules-check`, kit's Makefile no longer defines `rebuild-check`, and a test of `tests/kit/gate-targets.test.mts` reads the command of each. | `scripts/kit/kit.mk`, `Makefile` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/gate-targets.test.mts` | [o] |
| K248-2 | The parity check decides whether a repository needs a parity declaration (follows K248). Cause: without `config/parity.json` the check failed in every repository, so a repository with one implementation, such as kit, could not run the check that enforces S6. Acceptance: without the declaration a checkout whose `packages/` holds at most one implementation folder (`packages/<runtime>` or `packages/<component>/<runtime>`) passes with a line that says so, and one that holds two or more fails and names them. | `scripts/kit/parity.mts`, `scripts/kit/parity-declaration.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/parity.test.mts` | [o] |
| K248-2-1 | The parity declaration test creates its repositories through initRepository (follows K248-2). Cause: the K248-2 case ran `git init` itself and ignored the status of git, so the sandbox test failed in the CI run of e9d0bb8 and a failed git command went unseen. Acceptance: the case creates each repository with `initRepository` of `tests/kit/sandbox-repository.mts`, fails on a failed `git add`, and the sandbox test passes. | `tests/kit/parity.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/parity.test.mts tests/kit/sandbox-repository.test.mts` | [o] |
| K248-4 | kit's PHP lint has the autoloader of the PHP symbol extractor (follows K248). Cause: K248 added `make lint-php` to kit's CI, which runs PHPStan on `scripts/kit/symbols/php` with the autoloader of its Composer project, but nothing installs that project in kit, so the step failed in the run of e9d0bb8. Acceptance: kit's Makefile has `install-symbols-php`, which installs the locked packages into `scripts/kit/symbols/php/vendor` with the Composer of mise.toml; the CI workflow runs it after install-tools and before lint-php, which a test of the workflow checks; `make build` leaves that vendor/ out of dist/. | `Makefile`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `tests/source/ci-workflow.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/source/ci-workflow.test.mts tests/kit/kit-mk.test.mts`, `make lint-php` in CI | [o] |
| K248-4-1 | The PHP symbol extractor passes phpcs, and its vendor/ is no source (follows K248-4). Cause: with the packages that K248-4 installs, the CI run of 1785c5f ran phpcs on `extract.php` (a class without a namespace in a file that also runs logic, three lines over 120 characters), the release of the extractor read vendor/ as a file (EISDIR), and the build test expected vendor/ in dist/. Acceptance: the visitor is the class `Polyspec\Kit\Symbols\PublicSymbols` of `PublicSymbols.php`, which `extract.php` requires beside it, with the same output on the PHP packages of orm and template; phpcs passes on both files; `extractorRelease` skips vendor/ and fails on another folder; the build test compares dist/ with the tracked sources. | `scripts/kit/symbols/php/extract.php`, `scripts/kit/symbols/php/PublicSymbols.php`, `scripts/kit/install-symbols.mts`, `tests/source/package.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/install-symbols.test.mts`, `make lint-php` | [o] |
| K248-3 | Every repository runs the duplication check (follows K248). Cause: `make duplication-check` is a target of kit's own Makefile over `scripts/kit`, and jscpd is a development dependency of kit, so no consumer runs it while PRINCIPLES.md names it for the separation of concerns. Acceptance: a kit tool runs jscpd, a dependency of the package, over the tracked code of the checkout with the folders that `config/duplication.json` excludes, kit.mk defines `duplication-check`, the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names the check alone, and a test reads a clone and a clean tree. | `scripts/kit/duplication-check.mts`, `scripts/kit/kit.mk`, `package.json` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/duplication-check.test.mts` | [o] |
| K248-3-1 | The duplication check names the command of jscpd that it runs (follows K248-3). Cause: the isolation test of kit counts a dependency as used when a tool imports it or names its command as a string, and duplication-check.mts read the bin of jscpd as `bin.jscpd`, so the test failed on the dependency jscpd. Acceptance: the tool reads the bin through the constant `COMMAND`, `jscpd`, and the isolation test passes. | `scripts/kit/duplication-check.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/isolation.test.mts tests/kit/duplication-check.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249 | `make plan-new` writes the files of a plan. Cause: a rule that a person or an agent applies by hand is broken by a slip, and a check that refuses after the fact makes the work again; the plan, item and commit utilities of this item and its sub-items share the checks of the hooks, so their output passes them. Acceptance: `make plan-new NAME=<slug> TITLE= TITLE_KO=` writes plan.md, checklist.md and their Korean twins in `docs/plans/<slug>/` with their doc-id, language link, the header of the checklist table and the revision marker of each Korean file; it refuses a name that is no slug, an existing plan and an empty title, and writes nothing then; the values come from the environment, where make puts its command-line variables. | `scripts/kit/plan.mts`, `kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/plan.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249-1 | `make item-new` adds a row (follows K249). Acceptance: `make item-new PLAN= ID= TASK= TASK_KO= DELIVERABLES= VERIFICATION=` adds the row in the waiting state to the checklist of the plan and its Korean twin, after the last sub-item of its parent for an ID that ends in `-<n>` and at the end of the table otherwise, and writes only when the ID matches the pattern, is no item of any checklist, has its parent in that checklist, and the strict reading, the twin comparison and the cross check pass. | `scripts/kit/plan.mts`, `kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/plan.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249-2 | `make item-state` changes a state (follows K249). Acceptance: `make item-state ID= STATE=` sets the state in both languages only for a transition from waiting to active or impossible, from active to done, bypassed, impossible or waiting, and from bypassed to active; done needs a line with `(<ID>)` that the working tree adds to the changelog of both languages, bypassed needs CAUSE, RETRY, CAUSE_KO and RETRY_KO, impossible needs CAUSE and CAUSE_KO; it refuses while another item has a state other than that of HEAD, as the commit check would. | `scripts/kit/plan.mts`, `kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/plan.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249-3 | Each open item is verified by its own commands (follows K249). Acceptance: the Verification cell of an open item (waiting or active) holds at least one command in backticks and no command that the Claude Code hook refuses, so a suite or a run without a selection fails documents-check with the line; `make item-test ID=` runs each command of the cell in order and fails on the first that fails; the open items of kit's checklists name their unit tests. | `scripts/kit/plan.mts`, `scripts/kit/checklist.mts`, `kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/plan.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249-4 | `make commit` writes the commit of an item (follows K249). Acceptance: `make commit ID= TYPE= SCOPE= SUBJECT= BODY_FILE=` composes `type(scope): Subject (#ID)`, a blank line and the body of the file wrapped at the width of config/commits.json with its trailer lines kept, checks it with the functions of the commit-msg hook (the message and the item findings of the index) and commits it with git commit, which runs the hooks again. | `scripts/kit/plan.mts`, `kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/plan.test.mts` | [o] |
| K249-5 | An ID is a sub-item only when the part before its last -<number> is an ID of the pattern of its repository. Cause: every ID that ends in -<number> was read as a sub-item, so an ID scheme such as S-01 needed a parent S, which is no ID. | `scripts/kit/checklist.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/checklist.test.mts` | [o] |
| K241 | A plan is a folder with one plan and one checklist. Cause: every AGENTS.md allows one checklist per repository, which grows without bound, and kit checks item IDs within one checklist only. Acceptance: kit reads the checklists as `docs/plans/*/checklist.md`, each with `plan.md` and both Korean pairs beside it; a checklist without a plan, a plan without a checklist, another file in a plan folder, an item ID in two checklists and a sub-item whose parent is no item of its checklist fail with the file and line; `config/checklist.json` no longer lists paths, and kit fixes the table format and the five states, so its schema holds only the hooks and the ID pattern and refuses `states`, `format` and paths. An existing checklist is split by its plans: each section or wave becomes the folder of its plan with its rows unchanged, an existing plan document becomes its `plan.md`, a section without one gets a `plan.md` written from its heading and background, and no catch-all plan holds rows that belong to no plan; the move is checked by the equal sets of rows before and after it. | `scripts/kit/checklist.mts`, `scripts/kit/schema/checklist.schema.json`, `docs/plans/*/` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/checklist.test.mts tests/kit/documents.test.mts tests/kit/push-gate.test.mts tests/kit/commits.test.mts tests/source/checklist-real.test.mts` | [o] |
| K242 | A checklist row has the cells of its header. Cause: the repositories declared four or five states and the list or the table format; K241 fixed the table and the five states, but a row with a missing or extra cell is still read by position. Acceptance: a row whose number of cells differs from the header of its table fails with the file, the line and both counts, and the rows of kit's checklists that differ are fixed: an empty cell before the state is removed, a pipe in a code span is escaped, and a missing deliverables cell names the files of the item. | `scripts/kit/checklist.mts`, `scripts/kit/markdown.mts`, `docs/plans/*/checklist(.ko).md` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/checklist.test.mts tests/kit/markdown.test.mts`, `make documents-check` | [o] |
| K243 | The common rules of work are stated once. Cause: each AGENTS.md restates the commit, branch, checklist, test and idempotency rules in its own words. Acceptance: PRINCIPLES.md has a Workflow section that holds each rule that two or more AGENTS.md files state, kit's AGENTS.md keeps only kit's rules, and the commit types and limits are constants of the commit check. | `PRINCIPLES.md(.ko)`, `AGENTS.md(.ko)`, `scripts/kit/check-commits.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/commits.test.mts tests/kit/check-rules.test.mts`, `make rules-check` | [o] |
| K243-1 | Every rule of a rule document is a list item (follows K243). Cause: the rules check reads the list items of PRINCIPLES.md and AGENTS.md, and kit's AGENTS.md still states rules in prose under Purpose, Generated files and Release, which escape it. Acceptance: those rules are list items, each with its Enforced by line, and a check fails on a paragraph of a rule section of a rule document. | `AGENTS.md(.ko)`, `scripts/kit/check-rules.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/check-rules.test.mts`, `make rules-check` | [o] |
| K244 | Tests are classified by location and selected by one command. Cause: each repository names and selects its tests differently. Acceptance: a unit test stays where the runner of its ecosystem finds it in its package (S1), and the integration and conformance suites, which cross packages, live in `tests/integration/<runtime>/` and `tests/conformance/<runtime>/` of the root, which a layout rule requires; `make test-<runtime> CASES=<pattern>` runs the unit tests of each package of the runtime through run-tests with the name filter of its runner in the folder of the package; `make test-integration` and `make test-conformance` and a test target without CASES run only in CI, which the Claude Code hook enforces; a selection that runs no test fails. | `scripts/kit/runtime-targets.mts`, `kit.mk`, `config/layout.kit.yml`, `scripts/kit/claude-hook.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/runtime-targets.test.mts tests/kit/claude-hook.test.mts`, `make layout-check` | [o] |
| K244-1 | The rule suites-at-root applies. Cause: it lies among the templates of config/layout.kit.yml, which apply only where a rule extends them, so no checkout ran it, and the real layout test planted no suite in a package. | `config/layout.kit.yml`, `tests/source/layout-real.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node --test-name-pattern 'planted violation' -- tests/source/layout-real.test.mts` | [o] |
| K245 | The folder standard names one place for each concern. Cause: S5 allows `ts` and `js` for TypeScript sources, packages hold AGENTS, CHANGELOG and Makefile files, and the documents are laid out differently. Acceptance: the layout rules refuse a runtime folder that does not name its source language, a management file below the root, a test folder other than `tests/`, and a `docs/` layout other than `spec/`, `operations/`, `plans/` and `features.md`. | `config/layout.kit.yml`, `PRINCIPLES.md(.ko)` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/source/layout-real.test.mts tests/source/consumer-layout.test.mts`, `make layout-check` | [o] |
| K246 | The make targets have fixed names. Cause: only `check` and `install` exist in most repositories, and one task has up to four names. Acceptance: kit.mk defines the names `install`, `build`, `test`, `lint`, `docs-check` and `build-<runtime>`, `test-<runtime>`, `lint-<runtime>` for each folder of `packages/`; a check fails for a package without its targets and for a target that repeats one of these tasks under another name. | `kit.mk`, `scripts/kit/kit-check.mts`, `scripts/kit/runtime-targets.mts`, `scripts/kit/claude-hook.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/kit-check.test.mts tests/kit/claude-hook.test.mts tests/kit/runtime-targets.test.mts` | [o] |
| K246-1 | The package tests build kit with make kit-build, and the kit-check test names no other repository. Cause: K246 made make build the aggregate of the runtimes, which a copy without Git and the node of var/tools cannot run, so builtCopy, the package and consumer-layout tests failed in CI; the test of the runtime targets named orm in a path, which the isolation test refuses. | `tests/source/checkout.mts`, `tests/source/package.test.mts`, `tests/kit/kit-check.test.mts`, `tests/kit/php-ext.test.mts`, `package.json` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/isolation.test.mts tests/kit/kit-check.test.mts tests/source/checkout.test.mts` | [o] |
| K246-2 | A repository keeps its suite target check, and its install steps run after the bootstrap of kit. Cause: kit-check refused the target check, which is no fixed task but the suite that the Claude Code hook refuses locally; and a prerequisite of install ran before the recipe of kit, so the steps of a repository ran before npm and the package were installed. | `scripts/kit/kit-check.mts`, `scripts/kit/kit-bootstrap.mk`, `Makefile` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/kit-check.test.mts tests/kit/kit-bootstrap.test.mts` | [o] |
| K247 | A PHP extension follows one model and one build. Cause: the extensions follow five models, and repositories build the extensions of other repositories with scripts of their own. Acceptance: PRINCIPLES.md has S10: the extension registers the class names of the PHP library, lies in `packages/php-ext/src/` with one stub, is named `polyspec/<repository>-php-ext` and `polyspec_<repository>`, and is built by one kit tool with phpize, which generates its arginfo with gen_stub.php and fails when the stub hash in the header differs; the layout rules refuse another stub place or package name, and a kit declaration test fails when a class, method or signature that the extension registers differs from the PHP library. | `PRINCIPLES.md(.ko)`, `scripts/kit/php-extension.mts`, `scripts/kit/symbols/php/reflection.php`, `kit.mk`, `config/layout.kit.yml` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/php-ext.test.mts` | [o] |
| K247-1 | knip.json allows no binary that a repository may not use. Cause: K247 added ignoreBinaries php to the vendored knip.json, so knip of a repository that starts no php printed a configuration hint and the real lint test failed; the PHP extension test started php by its literal name, which knip lists, while the tool starts it through execute of process.mts. | `knip.json`, `tests/kit/php-ext.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/php-ext.test.mts tests/source/lint-ts-real.test.mts` | [o] |
| K252 | Code and commit messages are English by a check. Cause: PRINCIPLES.md requires English comments, identifiers, logs, error messages, test names and commit messages, and no check of kit reads them; a comment language check exists in one repository only. Acceptance: a kit check refuses Hangul in the comments, identifiers, error and log messages and test names of the tracked code, a TypeScript or JavaScript file read with its parser and a file of Go, Python, PHP or Rust by its comments and its code outside strings, while a string that is data, such as a phrase written into a Korean document, is not read; the commit check refuses it in commit messages; each finding names the file and line; it runs in every repository's CI, and the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names it. | `scripts/kit/language-check.mts`, `scripts/kit/check-commits.mts`, `scripts/kit/kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/language-check.test.mts` | [o] |
| K253 | The push gate holds the branch rules. Cause: PRINCIPLES.md names branches `{type}/{shortname}-{checklist id}` and worktrees `{project}-{shortname}-{checklist id}` and deletes them once merged into main, and no check reads them. Acceptance: the push gate refuses a pushed branch other than main whose name breaks the pattern, a worktree whose folder breaks its pattern, and a push while a local branch or worktree that is merged into main remains, naming each and the command that removes it; the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names the check. | `scripts/kit/push-gate.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/push-gate.test.mts` | [o] |
| K254 | No repository tracks a symbolic link that it creates. Cause: AGENTS.md of kit and the layout rules forbid the symbolic links that a repository creates, and no check reads the tracked files for them. Acceptance: a kit check refuses a tracked file of mode 120000 in the index and names it, it runs in every repository's CI, and the rule names it. | `scripts/kit/kit-check.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/kit-check.test.mts` | [o] |
| K255 | No test is skipped or narrowed by a marker. Cause: PRINCIPLES.md forbids lowering a standard while testing, and a skip or focus marker (`test.skip`, `it.only`, `t.Skip`, `markTestSkipped`, `#[ignore]`, `unittest.skip`, `pytest.mark.skip`) lowers it without a trace that a check reads. Acceptance: a kit check refuses each such marker in the tracked tests of every language, a TypeScript or JavaScript test read with its parser, so a marker inside a string is none, and a test of Go, Python, PHP or Rust by the marker on a line of code without its comment, naming the file and line; it runs in every repository's CI, and the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names it. | `scripts/kit/test-markers.mts`, `scripts/kit/kit.mk` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/test-markers.test.mts` | [o] |
| K256 | A swallowed error fails the lint of its language. Cause: PRINCIPLES.md forbids a hidden error, and the lint configurations that kit vendors do not all refuse an empty catch, an ignored error value or a bare except. Acceptance: the vendored configurations of oxlint, golangci-lint, ruff, PHPStan and clippy enable the rules of each ecosystem that refuse a swallowed error (an empty catch block, an unchecked error, a blind or silent except, an ignored result), each with a failing sample in the tests of kit, and the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names the lints. | `.oxlintrc.json`, `.golangci.yml`, `ruff.toml`, `phpcs.xml.dist`, `scripts/kit/lint-rust.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/lint-rust.test.mts tests/source/lint-ts-real.test.mts tests/source/lint-python-real.test.mts tests/source/lint-go-real.test.mts tests/source/lint-php-real.test.mts` | [o] |
| K257 | A fix commit carries its test. Cause: PRINCIPLES.md starts a defect with a test that reproduces it, and the commit check reads no file of a fix commit. Acceptance: the commit check refuses a commit of type `fix` that changes no test file of the runners that kit fixes (S3) and names the rule, the commit and the fix; the rule of PRINCIPLES.md names the check. | `scripts/kit/check-commits.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/commits.test.mts` | [o] |
| K257-1 | The commit check reads the type of a fix commit as oxlint requires (follows K257). Cause: K257 matched the type with the regular expression `/^fix\(/`, which the `prefer-string-starts-ends-with` rule of oxlint refuses, so `make lint-ts` failed after the push of 451f5a1. Acceptance: the check uses `startsWith`, and `make lint-ts` passes on `scripts/kit/check-commits.mts`. | `scripts/kit/check-commits.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/commits.test.mts`, `make lint-ts` | [o] |
| K258 | The Composer installer states what pins a package. Cause: `install-composer-project.mts` said that Composer checks the digest of each archive against the lock, but every `shasum` of kit's composer.lock files is empty, as Packagist gives for a GitHub archive, so Composer checks no digest and only the commit reference pins a package. Acceptance: the comment states the commit reference as the pin and that the lock holds no digest. | `scripts/kit/install-composer-project.mts` | `make lint-ts` | [o] |
| K250 | No test of kit names another repository or depends on the user or the wording of make, and lint-ts passes. Cause: the CI of main failed in lint-ts on an unused variable of `tests/kit/link.test.mts`; behind it the isolation test stopped at the first of eleven lines of `scripts/kit/test-output.mts`, `tests/kit/link.test.mts`, `tests/kit/parity.test.mts` and `tests/kit/test-output.test.mts` that named other repositories and missed fsm and flowmark in its list; two link tests matched the quotes and the location that make 3.81 prints, which make 4 prints otherwise; three read-only link tests expected a refusal that a user whom chmod does not stop, such as root, never gets. Acceptance: the texts and fixtures describe the behaviour without a repository name, the isolation list names every repository, the make tests assert the names that make prints and the stop of the recipe, the read-only tests assert the behaviour of the writability that link reads (`writable` of `scripts/kit/link.mts`), and lint-ts and the isolation, link and test-output tests pass; the case of a full parity-check in `tests/kit/parity.test.mts` still fails on the full-run gate, which K240 removes. | `scripts/kit/test-output.mts`, `scripts/kit/link.mts`, `tests/kit/isolation.test.mts`, `tests/kit/link.test.mts`, `tests/kit/parity.test.mts`, `tests/kit/test-output.test.mts` | `node scripts/kit/run-tests.mts node -- tests/kit/isolation.test.mts tests/kit/link.test.mts tests/kit/test-output.test.mts` | [o] |
| K250-1 | The export test of test-output reads a built copy (follows K250). Cause: the K217 case of `tests/kit/test-output.test.mts` imports `@polyspec/kit/test-output`, which resolves to dist/, but no CI step and no vendored run builds dist/, so the case failed in the run of e9d0bb8; the tests of kit's built package belong to tests/source, which builds a copy. | K566 | Parity declarations scope a feature only through an existing feature coverage owner or explicitly selected release artifact, while shared requirements remain bound to every implementation. Cause: mapping a component or artifact requirement to all clients makes requirement tracing overstate its applicability, while a free-form profile can erase a trace. Acceptance: Kit derives the owner from `contracts/features.json` or from the exact `archive`, `go-module` or `git-tag` declaration in `config/release.json`; the requirement names that owner as its subject and the native test names the requirement. Suites, API units and shared requirements cannot be scoped; unknown owners, mismatched package, artifact or part, unlisted tests and arbitrary overrides fail. A Cargo feature binding runs only its exact native `id`; a zero-match filter and any reported test outside the bound ids fail.
